# IEEE 1800 status audit matrix

This is the probe-led M7 audit record. Each row covers only the feature subset
named in its probe and evidence; a missing clause or subclause is unassessed.
Top-level `UNASSESSED` rows mark chapters with no probes yet; rows for a parent
clause never imply coverage of its children.
`UNASSESSED` means no clause-specific probe result is recorded.
`IMPLEMENTED` means every listed behavior produced its expected result in the
dedicated probes for both editions, including expected edition-specific
differences. `QUALIFIED` means the subset also has registered regression
coverage and has passed its required gate. `PARTIAL` means probes verified
both supported behavior and a gap; `UNSUPPORTED` means a required behavior
failed its probe or is absent. `N/A` means there is no independently testable
implementation requirement. No status claims full-clause conformance.
`PROBED — LEAD PENDING` means raw results are recorded but not yet classified
by the audit lead; it makes no conformance claim.

| §14.3 / §14.3 | Named clocking declaration, explicit event, default skews, clockvar directions, and drive/sample behavior pass probes in both editions (2017 pp. 336–338 / 2023 pp. 354–356). `output #0` is accepted in 2017 and compiles/runs in 2023; IEEE 1800-2017/2023 §14.4 explicitly permits `#0`, so this is not a gap. The §8.4 null-dereference result is not a gap: both editions allow an
indeterminate result and permit an implementation diagnostic without
requiring one. The event-control check in an ordinary function ([`n13_04_event_in_function.sv`](../../../tests/1800_audit/c08/neg/n13_04_event_in_function.sv)) is only a negative control, not a §13.4 qualification. The deeper RNG
stability rule is reserved for the later §18.14 audit. The §8.16 row records
only the tested `$cast` forms; interface-class sources and task-form runtime
error remain unprobed. §8.17 issue #619 tracks the confirmed
extends-arguments/explicit-`super.new()` gap. The §8 probes are not registered
regressions, so their passing subsets are `IMPLEMENTED`, not `QUALIFIED`. Any
unlisted features within a probed subclause remain unassessed.
